                                💡 AI Executive Summary: Key News Insights
                            </div>
                            <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" width="100%" style="background-color: #F8FAFC; border-left: 4px solid #FF9900; border: 1px solid #E2E8F0; border-left-width: 4px;">

                                    <td style="padding: 20px 24px; font-size: 13.5px; color: #334155; line-height: 1.7em; text-align: justify;">
                                        <div style='margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>1. 아마존 메릴랜드 690MW 원전 장기 PPA와 글로벌 전력 확보전</div>아마존(AWS)이 미국 최대 원전 기업 콘스텔레이션 에너지와 20년 장기 전력구매계약(PPA)을 체결하고 메릴랜드주 캘버트 클리프스 원전에서 690MW의 무탄소 전력을 확보함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiVEFVX3lxTE00OWd0dGhuaTkxSTZXQzZBM3dqRW1TMHFib3k0azZhVUN4RE83Ri1TbE4tZEFmRkRCM0VObmR4bG1fd01nSGVKS2luOWkzMDB3bmxieg?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a>. 동시에 30억 달러(약 4조 원) 규모의 원전 용량 증설을 지원하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiowFBVV95cUxNcjR1T0h3YzFGRE5CMXBxQnREdFd6UVVtU0E2c0htdFNJYTlmYWJPUTV1dnlHTHdpUkhXOXF4Sk0wcmxWQU5XSWNDSmdRTUdCbGxoWUJsSkNTYUstbkRzNU9QUUZmNVB2cHNGSEpXSi1zQUo1dl9zb0lfWmJmME13TXA4dl91NER5ODdBdjlTUmNDQjlJOVJBd1NiUXc0elJFVzM0?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a> AI 인프라 가동을 위한 기저전원을 선제적으로 구축함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiekFVX3lxTE5qSURPWG4ycEhhMFJ2RkpHWERvTk53QXd1ZU8wZXlJNDY2MnVKMzhiNm9vX1lxcGRqLTYzNEFQSG9PT2tJTmhxMVNZUU85cDJqNXhJN24ySFoxOWx4WGgtMmkyNDRXMi1rNFBob29nM29RRWNsdDAxUy1R0gGOAUFVX3lxTFBQX2VEUDU1a3dfcGRIT2tNdkxqX1JSOHljcUxEdnRGM2J4cmljYWJkOExNSGJGWlV1b2lrckM2R2ZPYkpCMGU3WVZETndvbFV2VG5RRTY4bnBZdV83U1ZCdjBCWVUyazZhS3VUUEFyUVVvT0taX2N0ZTFISk5tR251LTg2dmdrTGZKazBzVFE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a>. 아울러 2026년 설비투자(CapEx) 가이던스를 2,200억 달러(약 297조 원)로 상향 조정하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMimwFBVV95cUxPOHhNdzVocjhvLVQtQnZfam1PRjRZNGltdGt4R2I3bkI5V2I2M0RQUTUzUWFyMHZZX2hHcXU5Vkk5RlVNTzdwYkttQ2liRXZ1dnNXMEtqb2g5dG8xZ29CMV9OQktqampXY01DV00zWmhTZ0d0dzQwMC1wRXRDUXZDT2Q2ZjJSR0hZckZNSmZOd1o5ODF6Q0tOR0tfRQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a> 펜실베이니아 클라인 타운십의 인허가를 이끌어냄 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Amazon data center project in Schuylkill County approved unanimously by Kline Township supervisors&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a>. 글로벌 시장에서도 일본 JERA와 델(Dell)이 150억 달러(약 20조 원)를 투입해 400MW급 AI 센터 전력을 최장 25년간 공급받기로 합의했고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMickFVX3lxTE81Mm5nTUtpanBydjF2Tmw2SER3cEdJNW1GNHJZVi1HbmdTYUU2WmNsRGtXTGQ4UWg3b1RUYTRCZC11T2lfMkRlSmc5YmFVZEFQOW9ES0FGWG1pOHZqZEp6TmcyR2I1Ynlxc1F6djFxUmpZQQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a> 미시간 규제당국은 구글 데이터센터 특별 전력 계약을 승인함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiqwFBVV95cUxPaFF4NkZ1V2ZBQjc5VUVSOFFXbTVsaWUtX1RPVUVoZHZ5V1F5WjRLNXl4bzFzWEVDNGozZXAzYnlvX0hzMGx1LVFNQlh3N0Rub3V2bnZXYm4wX2ZmQ2gyaGJvX1pTUXlyT1ltMVFENS00Z1VEa21rUkcxdW1tZDV2MTExY000R3Rpc1QtY1AzTmlSMlV2RnRPZWhZUHY4ZmNReUJ0azRJTFE1Q3c?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[7]</a>. 국내에서는 한전과 SK하이닉스가 용인 클러스터 안정을 위해 2.5GW 정전 제로 동맹을 맺고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE8wMThodE4zT05ydE40MldpZEplQXBwTXpvU2pHcXk1b1RBcmMtbDRyTU1BaTNPQ1gzSjdZT2E5LW1Gal83bEZ1UjYwS0dreDBDMVZBeVEwbHhxbENXZjFKa0NrN08?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a>, 정부가 미국 텍사스 '프로젝트 스타' 가스발전에 223억 달러(약 30조 원) 전략 투자를 단행하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibkFVX3lxTE5ZamcwM2lKbDZELUpsZFprZFVaT1VjR2c1ejBXN0hrVmpkNk5CNE43dlRpUmhFTlNBelZWNlJKX0dIVV84cndJQlZzRHVHODBaQ2RyRUdyVDFLUTNndWxfWHlpbXB6ZVc3TWkzLVdn?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a> 기저전원 직접 소유 및 장기 조달이 인프라 확장의 핵심 축으로 자리 잡음 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMif0FVX3lxTE15b2RYUkdSRDdnME4zanVmcmx0WGtfMk9iVDhWR08yTjhkX2Fhb2NtSUVqOUs5anlxNHJOUTNDOHhtUk1GM0FFMjlzWUR4anBjVmdrc081WmR5NzZKVTlKMTU5VUdrTXQ5Wmd2cHptbnJ4ZkFGbTBTQjlydVppcjTSAZMBQVVfeXFMT3VncW1McjlhYnJEYThXclRfYkJkenBZVnpTWkxPdTVnQjdjM3VzbVNvQlVsSndEYW84b1lhQWNPMmNDTzZpVUJWTzBjaGNYY0RtVGdtMkZVVFMtOGlmRGR0eWFJRFNwN2VmRHJZSnBDbGIzbjk5YURteDN6Q1hQZEtuaGh4ODBtMXk2S29YVFhQNHhB?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a>.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>2. LG전자 버지니아 칠러 공장 신설과 엔비디아 인증 액체냉각 생태계</div>초고밀도 랙의 발열 제어가 AIDC의 핵심 과제로 대두되자 LG전자가 1,500억 원을 투자해 미국 버지니아주에 AI 데이터센터 전용 공랭식 칠러 공장을 신설하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE5lU24xcEdzYTgtTVdxemUwbFhwaHFsc054b0tUejFDQ1VOeG1YZVYxLU56WVM3T2RtUUxGUTlHTlJrd08yMjc3VmowSWZUX2F0QTBrV0o3TzczMEVXTm1PMm1TYUM?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a> 평택과 창원의 국내 생산라인 증설을 동시 추진함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiggFBVV95cUxPS05OR2JFZFlZQWpmN21DNUpCX2ltQVhia09EUHZQSW1pVVhnV1hEZW9XZld5VFR6T053UG84cDFBM29KcklvT1VuRzljMGc1MkpLMURpenRzdzVrQlNoMkJ2c0xfYzRuNXphSkRMLU8wOVQyVE1lNm03ZDJhY2lqR3hB0gGWAUFVX3lxTFBuTXpobE9fc2Z4QkdCX1g4Y2RwYVJmakJZQjFPNEc3VHIxWU1IbFNMNm1XM1BvcnF1U2JjVDBvYVRxZlNiTzA2ZU5tRDd0Z0RSelVDTjFSdGlTd0VBd0R2Qm96aS16OFJieUJncV8zQ0hjOGY3dTUtdUp3b25TcGkzYWpUNldJdHRYdklxMkQtV1lOQ09oQQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[12]</a>. 아울러 LG전자는 엔비디아 파트너 네트워크(NPN)의 냉각 솔루션 파트너로 선정되며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiaEFVX3lxTE5INllHV2ZFcjYxai1ieF9rcGQ0dEhUTUt3MVFtdWI0Um5Fc3h5OWlrcTJuVVRhT0ZHelppVlBHa3N2THFRME05ZlJFN2JaSGJvT0VWbHFkNndMT0tnN0hJQjczeXRjblB6?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a> 칠러부터 냉각분배장치(CDU)까지 결합한 '칩 투 칠러' 턴키 공급망을 구축함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMia0FVX3lxTE12SHNJYzM1VnFLQnNDRDFiUUhkTUpNREJIcVFJdnlLRHRERVY5ODJFSEVHb0dsQjVNaWExSzhObjI4QkdEZ2sybjFnVlBibEtBUkhUem8tUHFNcHE2eVRFb3o3UE9NNHFSNWpV0gFrQVVfeXFMTXZIc0ljMzVWcUtCc0NEMWJRSGRNSk1EQkhxUUl2eUtEdERFVjk4MkVIRUdvR2xCNU1pYTFLOE5uMjhCR0RnazJuMWdWUGJsS0FSSFR6by1QcU1wcTZ5VEVvejdQT000cVI1alU?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a>. 국내 냉각 기업 오텍캐리어 역시 1.3MW급 액체냉각 CDU에 대해 엔비디아 공식 인증을 획득하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMilgFBVV95cUxNUXhIVkF5ZDJmSTgtZ1FMSFBsVDNrLXB5Znc0akh3QjhHRzBjeUlYNDZteHdSTDVzeE5aS20td3N2cDJLbVdIUFlsYUVrZFZmcURmYnNYNFlRcGRWVHYzQW9Cd0RGQmtKSXVFbHpxMGtjNWRpUk00VUFkTHJLS01wQnVUZ1BoV0g0V015bU9wVnJvRUgtVXfSAZYBQVVfeXFMTVF4SFZBeWQyZkk4LWdRTEhQbFQzay1weWZ3NGpId0I4R0cwY3lJWDQ2bXh3Ukw1c3hOWkttLXdzdnAyS21XSFBZbGFFa2RWZnFEZmJzWDRZUXBkVlR2M0FvQndERkJrSkl1RWx6cTBrYzVkaVJNNFVBZExyS0tNcEJ1VGdQaFdINFdNeW1PcFZyb0VILVV3?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a> 연내 2.6MW급 차세대 제품 출시를 공식화하며 아시아 허브 선점에 돌입함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiekFVX3lxTE0yZEp5WnRRSVFBdUtBR1RjWEozVEYzQ1VybkVKakJlLWphUVlfcl9pTUhPakJPWjF2cXIxOXhiNklyZXAzR3RmTUtyaU9UaUtuOF9ZZm40amdtQU0wS3p0aC1UenlVQ0IxemRkaUhTZTJ5ZXhMQVFVNlZn0gGOAUFVX3lxTE9ySjFDVVdCYjEtemlTTGpxQ2ptNHZQYnB6S0ZSa3FHNUlwZE52emlvT3VpbzFyaEx6cGJoOWs5VWVxSTB5Mk1pak5jWmdZemtCUXdBcTllM3NmTmxXSFBnR0FvXzV2VXMyejFOTTA5b1VFM3RrTWJZVVdibWZlR3JtaTZjUTkwUlUwazNyaFE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7;
